<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">                      style</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">{ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"--code-l"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: t.light, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"--code-d"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: t.dark </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> t.light } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">CSSProperties</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">              {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> lines.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">\n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Seconds elapsed while `active` is true; freezes when it turns false. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> useElapsed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">active</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">elapsed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">setElapsed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useEffect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">active) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> window.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">setInterval</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> setElapsed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((performance.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">now</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> start) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> window.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">clearInterval</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(id)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> elapsed</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Formats seconds as `420ms`, `2.4s` or `1m 05s`. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> formatDuration</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">seconds</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Number.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">isFinite</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(seconds) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> seconds </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "0s"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (seconds </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Math</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">seconds</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}ms`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (seconds </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">seconds</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">toFixed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}s`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (seconds </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 60</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Math</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">seconds</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}s`</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">m</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}m ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">padStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"0"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}s`</span></span>
